31:Tcbd09,<h1 id="json-contracts">JSON contracts</h1>
<h2 id="one-versioned-system-schema">One versioned system schema</h2>
<p>insonic uses <a href="https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12">JSON Schema Draft 2020-12</a> for configuration, import, artifact/metadata interchange, portable catalog snapshots, durable job events, graph queries and speaker-training/model manifests. A system-wide master schema contains a registry of all insonic-owned contracts and selects documents through their <code>kind</code>. Shared definitions provide consistent identifiers, digests, revisions and artifact references. Cueson retains its own immutable upstream subtitle schema; the master recording embeds its validated current document without redefining that format. Its UUID assignments appear only there; external speaker mappings and other contracts carry current document/cue references, without another assignment list. Local packaged upstream schema bytes provide offline validation.</p>
<p>The master and child schemas live in <code>schemas/v&lt;version&gt;/</code>. Every release has the same software, documentation and master-schema version. A document declares <code>schema_version</code> and <code>kind</code>; the registry resolves its exact child contract. Tagged canonical schema IDs identify release resources, and the packaged registry resolves them offline. Schema files carry descriptions and examples for fields and complete documents. The documentation build reads those fields to produce the contract reference below and packages the JSON files beside it.</p>
<h2 id="compatibility-and-evolution">Compatibility and evolution</h2>
<p>Portable workspace backups use the registered <code>workspace-backup</code> contract. Its manifest binds a consistent logical catalog proof to exact current/durable artifact publications, verified copied bytes or explicit original-store dependency, and accepted-evidence graph rebuilding. Structural validation does not substitute for byte hashes, source receipt validation, destination ownership checks or retention lifetime. See <a href="/docs/v1.0.1/storage/#portable-backup-commands">backup commands</a>.</p>
<p>Release candidates use the registered <code>release-candidate</code> contract. The manifest records all four Windows/Linux platform/variant packages, corresponding-source and signing dispositions, exact source revision and documentation archives. Release tooling verifies the embedded package inventories, byte hashes and source identity before publication. See <a href="/docs/v1.0.1/releases/">release preparation</a>.</p>
<p>Model selection separates revisioned convenience aliases and configured source catalogs from immutable elected identities. Runtime requests expose alias/source CRUD, discovery and resolution; processing/import payloads freeze selected bundle manifests and acquisition dependencies. Optional compatibility metadata augments base manifests without rewriting accepted older digests. Portable catalog records retain alias/source revisions and trained/hosted target distinctions. Structural schema validation complements runtime checks for target ownership, complete bundle compatibility and job fences. See <a href="/docs/v1.0.1/models/">models</a> for executable commands and lifecycle.</p>
<p>Recording replacement is a targeted media election with explicit transcript keep, clear or replace and declared-roster retain or clear policies. Keep requires an applicability assertion and validates known source-clock bounds and selected track/channel compatibility. A retained older managed subtitle can become the validated embedded current document during that same acceptance. Failure preserves previous accepted state. Roster read and edit requests carry independent optimistic revision authority; intentional membership remains distinct from document attribution and training evidence. The runtime request and <a href="/docs/v1.0.1/ingestion/">import rules</a> expose these elections consistently.</p>
<p>Preserve backward and forward compatibility where practical. Prefer additive optional fields, stable meanings, explicit extension containers and migrations over silently changing existing values. Readers validate against the document's declared version and negotiate adapter capabilities. An unknown version is reported clearly; it is not silently interpreted with whichever schema is newest. Keep supported older registries available for imports and migrations. Unsupported features can remain preserved as opaque extension values where the contract permits them.</p>
<p>Compatibility is a priority, not an absolute prohibition on useful change. When a change breaks a consumer or persisted contract, describe the affected fields, supported source versions and migration/recovery path in the changelog and the brief release highlights. Release notes retain the final changelog link. A schema revision alone does not prove compatibility; validation and representative import/export/migration fixtures establish the supported combinations.</p>
<h2 id="validation-and-documentation">Validation and documentation</h2>
<p>Speaker-work requests distinguish dataset creation/inspection, elected training, exact speaker-version retrieval, profile CAS updates and matching-only work. Public matching input cannot supply server-frozen recording/model digests or replace the declared roster with a cluster-count hint. Zero score thresholds and ambiguity margins are explicit values. Catalog schema 9 carries portable output/profile/checkpoint authority and origin-qualified mappings; model/dataset manifests validate against their exact child contracts.</p>
<p>The repository checks master registration, matching release identities, schema validity, field descriptions and embedded examples. Every document example must validate both against its child schema and the master. Contract checks also reject malformed identities, contradictory inputs and mismatched kinds/versions. Structural validation is complemented by application checks for source ownership, time bounds, permissions, revision ordering and backend capabilities; JSON Schema cannot prove those relationships by itself.</p>
<p>The generated reference is read-only output. Edit descriptions, examples and field constraints in the authoritative JSON files, then rebuild documentation. The same packaged definitions serve CLI validation, adapter interchange and offline help. <a href="/docs/v1.0.1/schema/">Catalog relationships</a>, <a href="/docs/v1.0.1/ingestion/">import behavior</a> and <a href="/docs/v1.0.1/voice-models/">speaker model lineage</a> explain the domain rules around these payloads.</p>
<h2 id="contract-reference">Contract reference</h2>
<p>The versioned root of all insonic-owned interchange contracts. Each document identifies its kind and system schema version. Software, documentation and this registry share the same release version. Stable required fields define each operation while documented extension containers support adapter-specific values. Cueson documents retain their separate immutable upstream schema rather than being rewritten as an insonic format.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/master.schema.json">Download the master schema</a> · <a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/common.schema.json">Download shared definitions</a></p>
<p>The following definitions, descriptions and examples are generated from the versioned JSON Schema files during the documentation build.</p>
<h2 id="insonic-immutable-artifact-manifest">insonic immutable artifact manifest</h2>
<p>Portable inventory of complete immutable bytes. Hosted model handles without downloadable bytes belong in the speaker-model contract and are not invented digest entries here.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/artifact-manifest.schema.json">Download artifact-manifest.schema.json</a></p>

<h3 id="artifacts">artifacts</h3>
<p>Complete artifact entries. Application validation additionally rejects repeated artifact IDs with conflicting content identity.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="artifacts-1">artifacts[]</h3>
<p>One complete immutable artifact with portable byte identity and optional verified durable replicas.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>

<td><code>artifact_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<h3 id="artifactslocations">artifacts[].locations</h3>
<p>Verified durable locations when available. Availability and leases are runtime state, separate from content identity.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="artifactslocations-1">artifacts[].locations[]</h3>
<p>Durable managed object location. Keys identify exact objects; credentials and cached materialization paths never appear here.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>profile</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/backendProfileReference</td>
<td>Profile selected to read this object.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>storage_type</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>&quot;filesystem&quot;, &quot;s3&quot;</td>
<td>Official storage API used for the durable location.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>key</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>bucket</code></td>
<td>No</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
<p>All of the following constraints apply:</p>
<p>When <code>storage_type</code> is &quot;s3&quot;:</p>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>bucket</code>.</p>
<p>Otherwise:</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>bucket</code>.</p>

<h2 id="insonic-downloaded-base-model-manifest">insonic downloaded base model manifest</h2>
<p>Exact downloaded model identity, upstream revision, declared capabilities/license and ordered file digests. Registration does not claim local availability. Acquisition verifies every declared file before an available installation is committed. Credentials remain opaque references and never appear in URLs.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/base-model-manifest.schema.json">Download base-model-manifest.schema.json</a></p>

<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>

<h2 id="portable-catalog-snapshot">Portable catalog snapshot</h2>
<p>Consistent logical catalog export with typed provenance and ordered operational rows. Restoration verifies digest, workspace, supported migration version and all relationships, admits only an empty destination and expires imported authority. It does not copy artifact bytes or activate a different profile.</p>

<h3 id="definition-termvariants">Definition: term.variants</h3>
<p>Ordered variants, deduplicated only during context compilation.</p>

<p>Explicit recognition variant.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="definition-termspeakerid">Definition: term.speaker_id</h3>
<p>Optional known-speaker link.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-termspeakerid-alternative-1">Definition: term.speaker_id: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Optional known-speaker link.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid.</p>
<h3 id="definition-termspeakerid-alternative-2">Definition: term.speaker_id: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>
<h3 id="definition-termaliasid">Definition: term.alias_id</h3>
<p>Optional alias link.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-termaliasid-alternative-1">Definition: term.alias_id: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Optional alias link; must belong to the linked speaker when both are supplied.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid.</p>
<h3 id="definition-termaliasid-alternative-2">Definition: term.alias_id: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>

<h2 id="insonic-saved-graph-query">insonic saved graph query</h2>
<p>Immutable query definitions with typed parameters and declared backend compatibility. Every execution resolves current sources; presentation state is separate from evidence.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/graph-query.schema.json">Download graph-query.schema.json</a></p>

<td>Document discriminator registered by the master schema.</td>

</tr>

<h3 id="definition">definition</h3>
<p>Exactly one normalized application specification or native dialect/text definition.</p>
<p>Exactly one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-normalized">definition: normalized</h3>
<p>Portable application QuerySpec compiled separately by each official graph adapter.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>mode</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;normalized&quot;</td>
<td>Selects backend-neutral application operations.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>operation</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>&quot;media-list&quot;, &quot;speaker-search&quot;, &quot;model-list&quot;, &quot;text-search&quot;, &quot;time-range&quot;, &quot;evidence-traverse&quot;, &quot;graph-view&quot;</td>
<td>Supported normalized operation with identical intended results on LadybugDB and ArcadeDB.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>filters</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>object</td>
<td>Portable application filters. Unknown filters are rejected instead of ignored.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>traversal</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>object</td>
<td>Explicit bounded graph expansion, without an engine-specific query string.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>order_by</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>array</td>
<td>Deterministic ordering, with a stable entity-ID tie breaker added by the application if needed.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>pagination</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>object</td>
<td>Bounded current-result pagination.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>

<h3 id="example-4">Example</h3>

<h2 id="insonic-import-manifest">insonic import manifest</h2>
<p>Batch import input for the CLI and its GUI wrapper. Syntax validation precedes application validation of source access, calendar values, timezone/DST policy and admission. No approval per item is required for already configured routing.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/import-manifest.schema.json">Download import-manifest.schema.json</a></p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>schema_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;1.0.1&quot;</td>
<td>Version of the insonic document contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>kind</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;import-manifest&quot;</td>
<td>Document discriminator used by the master registry.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>defaults</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/options</td>
<td>Manifest defaults inherited after a selected saved preset and before each item.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>items</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>array</td>
<td>Inputs in deterministic admission order, with independent partial-result reporting.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Optional namespaced additions preserved without changing the core contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>overrides</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/options</td>
<td>Explicit invocation options overriding defaults and every item; absent fields inherit.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
<h3 id="items">items</h3>
<p>Inputs in deterministic admission order, with independent partial-result reporting.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="items-1">items[]</h3>
<p>Recording-date defaults and policies. Exact timestamp, calendar date and approximate UTC range are mutually exclusive.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>source</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Local source path or acquisition-adapter source locator. Credentials and temporary signed URLs must be resolved separately.</td>
<td>&quot;audio/session.wav&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>subtitle</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Compatibility alias for transcript, with the same independent acquisition and validation contract.</td>
<td>&quot;video/session.srt&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>originated_at</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/wallTimestamp</td>
<td>Entered recording timestamp, distinct from import, publication and file modification times.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>originated_on</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/calendarDate</td>
<td>Entered recording date when the recording time is unknown.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>timezone</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/timezone</td>
<td>Timezone for unzoned recording timestamps; local is resolved once for the batch.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>dst_fold</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>&quot;earlier&quot;, &quot;later&quot;</td>
<td>Explicit choice of offset for an ambiguous repeated daylight-saving wall time.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>dst_gap</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Constant &quot;shift-forward&quot;</td>
<td>Explicit policy to resolve a nonexistent wall time by shifting forward; absent means no silent correction.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>copy</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>boolean</td>
<td>True admits a managed copy into the selected artifact store; false keeps an explicitly tracked local reference.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>preset</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Saved import preset exact name or UUID. A missing/deleted selection fails before work admission.</td>
<td>&quot;Daily recordings&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Optional nonsecret input annotations.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>title</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Optional library display title; source identity still comes from verified bytes.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>new_entry</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>boolean</td>
<td>Explicitly create another library entry even when this source was already admitted. Identical bytes can share the source asset.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>credential_id</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>
<td>Workspace-scoped acquisition credential reference, resolved privately by the configured adapter.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>acquisition_adapter</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Explicit configured acquisition adapter; absent selects the source URI scheme without changing providers on failure.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>date_precedence</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>&quot;owner-first&quot;, &quot;embedded-first&quot;, &quot;filesystem-fallback&quot;</td>
<td>Versioned recording-date precedence rule. Filesystem modification becomes a recording-date candidate only under the explicit filesystem-fallback rule.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>originated_earliest</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/utcInstant</td>
<td>Inclusive earliest recording instant for an owner-supplied approximate date range. Preserve the exact UTC timestamp and integer clock.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>originated_latest</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/utcInstant</td>
<td>Inclusive latest recording instant for an owner-supplied approximate date range. Application validation checks bound ordering.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>local_http</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>boolean</td>
<td>Explicitly elect HTTP credential transport for a loopback endpoint only. Credentials otherwise require HTTPS; unauthenticated HTTP sources remain supported.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>acquisition_max_bytes</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Maximum acquired original bytes, default 64 GiB. Declared and streamed lengths are bounded before publication.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>acquisition_timeout_ms</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Positive total acquisition deadline in milliseconds, default ten minutes. Per-item values override batch defaults.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>transcript_max_bytes</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Independent transcript acquisition and document byte ceiling (maximum 16 MiB).</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>transcript_timeout_ms</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Independent transcript acquisition timeout in milliseconds.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>attribution</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>&quot;auto&quot;, &quot;native&quot;, &quot;off&quot;, &quot;diarize&quot;</td>
<td>Preserve existing assignments; derive structured participation only when none exist. Diarize explicitly elects configured acoustic processing without recognition.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>transcript_format</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>&quot;cueson&quot;, &quot;srt&quot;, &quot;vtt&quot;, &quot;ass&quot;, &quot;ssa&quot;</td>
<td>Explicit native or Cue JSON format hint validated against input bytes.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>replace_transcript</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>boolean</td>
<td>Elect replacement when the target already has a current transcript.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>subtitle_stream_index</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Absolute embedded subtitle stream index to select.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>subtitle_language</code></td>

</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>diarization_model_id</code></td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>transcript</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Independent local or authorized acquisition locator for native subtitles or Cue JSON. Explicit invalid input fails; it never falls back to embedded/generated text.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>record</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Existing recording UUID or unambiguous exact title for standalone transcript admission.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>transcript_credential_id</code></td>

<td></td>

<td></td>

<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<h3 id="itemsknownspeakers">items[].known_speakers</h3>
<p>Initial declared identities by UUID or unique exact canonical name/active alias. Empty declares an empty roster.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="itemsknownspeakers-1">items[].known_speakers[]</h3>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="itemspipelineid">items[].pipeline_id</h3>
<p>Configured pipeline UUID. Empty explicitly selects admission only.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="itemspipelineid-alternative-1">items[].pipeline_id: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Value: Constant &quot;&quot;.</p>
<h3 id="itemspipelineid-alternative-2">items[].pipeline_id: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid.</p>
<p>Exactly one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="items-media">items[]: media</h3>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>kind</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;media&quot;</td>
<td>Selected input discriminator.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>transcript</code>, <code>subtitle</code>.</p>
<h3 id="items-alternative-2">items[]: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Required fields: <code>source</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<h3 id="items-alternative-3">items[]: Alternative 3</h3>
<p>Required fields: <code>record</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: <code>kind</code> is &quot;media&quot;.</p>
<p>Exactly one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="items-alternative-3-alternative-1">items[]: Alternative 3: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Required fields: <code>source</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<h3 id="items-alternative-3-alternative-2">items[]: Alternative 3: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Required fields: <code>transcript</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<h3 id="items-alternative-3-alternative-3">items[]: Alternative 3: Alternative 3</h3>
<p>Required fields: <code>subtitle</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>

<td>Positive total acquisition deadline in milliseconds, default ten minutes. Per-item values override batch defaults.</td>
<td></td>

<td></td>

<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<h3 id="definition-optionsknownspeakers">Definition: options.known_speakers</h3>
<p>Initial declared identities by UUID or unique exact canonical name/active alias. Empty declares an empty roster.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="definition-optionsknownspeakers-1">Definition: options.known_speakers[]</h3>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="definition-optionspipelineid">Definition: options.pipeline_id</h3>
<p>Configured pipeline UUID. Empty explicitly selects admission only.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-optionspipelineid-alternative-1">Definition: options.pipeline_id: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Value: Constant &quot;&quot;.</p>
<h3 id="definition-optionspipelineid-alternative-2">Definition: options.pipeline_id: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid.</p>

<h2 id="insonic-durable-job-event">insonic durable job event</h2>
<p>Versioned ordered event for a durable job/attempt. Event syntax does not establish a committed catalog or graph result: publication receipts/checkpoints and application state transitions retain that authority.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/job-event.schema.json">Download job-event.schema.json</a></p>

<h2 id="insonic-metadata-extraction-tools">insonic metadata extraction tools</h2>
<p>Workspace metadata extractor configuration. Every configured executable has an absolute selected path, exact version and SHA-256. Hash/version qualification happens before extraction, and child processes use hidden noninteractive I/O. Missing or failed extractors record explicit capture outcomes while retaining usable original media.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/media-tools.schema.json">Download media-tools.schema.json</a></p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>kind</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;media-tools&quot;</td>
<td>Workspace extractor-configuration discriminator.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>schema_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;1.0.1&quot;</td>
<td>Insonic contract release version.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>exiftool</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/tool</td>
<td>Selected embedded-metadata extractor, preserving qualified groups, repeated tags and structured values.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>ffprobe</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/tool</td>
<td>Selected complementary stream/container probe, supplying measured source duration and clock facts.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>ffmpeg</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: #/$defs/tool</td>
<td>Optional exact mapped-audio decoder required only by selected processing operations.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
<h3 id="definition-tool">Definition: tool</h3>
<p>One exact local executable selected for metadata extraction.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>

</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>path</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Absolute executable path on this machine; executable selection is explicit and never inferred from an ambient PATH.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>sha256</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Exact expected upstream version reported by the selected executable.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>support_files</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>array</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td>No</td>

<h2 id="insonic-early-media-metadata-snapshot">insonic early media metadata snapshot</h2>

<h3 id="observationsgrouppath">observations[].group_path</h3>
<p>Ordered qualified group path retaining otherwise colliding tag names.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="observationsgrouppath-1">observations[].group_path[]</h3>
<p>One instance-qualified metadata group component.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="observationsrawvalue">observations[].raw_value</h3>
<p>Original JSON-representable value without replacing it with the normalized interpretation.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="observationsrawvalue-alternative-1">observations[].raw_value: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="observationsrawvalue-alternative-2">observations[].raw_value: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: number.</p>
<h3 id="observationsrawvalue-alternative-3">observations[].raw_value: Alternative 3</h3>
<p>Value: boolean.</p>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>

<h3 id="definition-entity">Definition: entity</h3>
<p>Stable saved pipeline with monotonic revision. Expected revision controls mutation; new input revision may be omitted or zero.</p>

<h3 id="definition-configuration-1">Definition: configuration</h3>
<p>Nonsecret optional provider settings.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>enabled</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>boolean</td>
<td>Explicit provider election.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>adapter</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>&quot;insonic-http&quot;, &quot;ollama&quot;</td>
<td>Versioned adapter contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>contract_version</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Constant &quot;1&quot;</td>
<td>Provider contract version.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>route</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>&quot;local&quot;, &quot;hosted&quot;</td>
<td>Explicit election. insonic-http local requires loopback. Native Ollama local requires observed local inference, including configured LAN hosts; hosted permits explicitly elected cloud/connected models. Endpoint scope is separately reported.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>endpoint</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Absolute elected HTTP(S) destination.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>model</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Provider model identifier.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>credential_id</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Opaque credential reference; no secret values.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>mode</code></td>

<h2 id="insonic-current-recording">insonic current recording</h2>
<p>Current embedded Cueson subtitle and recording-local speaker assignments with revision-fenced external known-speaker correlations. Names, aliases and vocabulary remain independent current catalog domains; assignments are resolved only from the current document. Replacements invalidate dependent corpus and preparation references while preserving originals and noncontent model lineage.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/recording.schema.json">Download recording.schema.json</a></p>

<h2 id="verified-native-release-candidate">Verified native release candidate</h2>
<p>Exact-version complete native package and documentation inventory. Structural validation complements source revision, corresponding-source, signing, checksum and publication readback checks. Synthetic examples illustrate shape and do not represent a shipped release.</p>

<h3 id="installerssigning">installers[].signing</h3>
<p>MSI signing is independent of contained executables and is explicitly unsigned.</p>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>verified</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant false</td>
<td>A signed payload does not establish an MSI publisher signature.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>status</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;unconfigured&quot;</td>
<td>This MSI remains independently unsigned.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>

  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;packages&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span>
    <span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
      <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;kind&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;native-package-receipt&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>

<h2 id="runtime-request">Runtime request</h2>
<p>A bounded local runtime operation correlated to a workspace and request. Job operations use durable catalog attempts and idempotent acceptance.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/runtime-request.schema.json">Download runtime-request.schema.json</a></p>

<p>All of the following constraints apply:</p>
<p>When the condition matches:</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>after_generation</code>.</p>
<p>When <code>operation</code> is &quot;jobs.start&quot;:</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>duration_ms</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>job_id</code>.</p>
<p>When <code>operation</code> matches &quot;jobs.show&quot;, &quot;jobs.cancel&quot;, &quot;jobs.retry&quot;, &quot;jobs.history&quot;:</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>job_id</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>duration_ms</code>.</p>
<p>When <code>operation</code> matches &quot;workspace.show&quot;, &quot;doctor&quot;, &quot;catalog.show&quot;:</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<p>When <code>operation</code> matches &quot;workspace.show&quot;, &quot;doctor&quot;, &quot;catalog.show&quot;, &quot;jobs.start&quot;, &quot;jobs.show&quot;, &quot;jobs.cancel&quot;, &quot;jobs.retry&quot;, &quot;jobs.history&quot;:</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<p>When <code>operation</code> matches &quot;artifacts.publish&quot;:</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>source_path</code>, <code>artifact_kind</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<p>When <code>operation</code> matches &quot;artifacts.lease-renew&quot;, &quot;artifacts.lease-release&quot;:</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>publication_id</code>, <code>lease_id</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<p>When <code>operation</code> matches &quot;artifacts.retain&quot;, &quot;artifacts.release-reference&quot;:</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>publication_id</code>, <code>reference_id</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<p>When <code>operation</code> matches &quot;artifacts.show&quot;, &quot;artifacts.verify&quot;, &quot;artifacts.materialize&quot;, &quot;artifacts.reconcile&quot;, &quot;artifacts.abort&quot;, &quot;artifacts.retire&quot;, &quot;artifacts.cache-prune&quot;:</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>publication_id</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: the condition matches.</p>
<p>When <code>operation</code> matches Structured value:</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>max_bytes</code>.</p>
<p>When <code>operation</code> matches &quot;workspace.show&quot;, &quot;doctor&quot;, &quot;catalog.show&quot;, &quot;jobs.start&quot;, &quot;jobs.show&quot;, &quot;jobs.cancel&quot;, &quot;jobs.retry&quot;, &quot;jobs.history&quot;, &quot;artifacts.publish&quot;, &quot;artifacts.show&quot;, &quot;artifacts.verify&quot;, &quot;artifacts.materialize&quot;, &quot;artifacts.reconcile&quot;, &quot;artifacts.abort&quot;, &quot;artifacts.retire&quot;, &quot;artifacts.lease-renew&quot;, &quot;artifacts.lease-release&quot;, &quot;artifacts.retain&quot;, &quot;artifacts.release-reference&quot;, &quot;artifacts.cache-prune&quot;:</p>

<h3 id="value-4">value</h3>
<p>Exact signed 64-bit integer as a JSON integer or canonical decimal string; the runtime enforces range.</p>
<p>Exactly one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="value-alternative-1-3">value: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Value: integer.</p>

</code></pre>

<h2 id="runtime-response">Runtime response</h2>
<p>A versioned operation result or redacted typed error. Early CLI errors omit unavailable identities; runtime replies include the workspace, request and session correlation.</p>

<h2 id="speaker-training-dataset-snapshot">Speaker training dataset snapshot</h2>

<h3 id="exclusions-1">exclusions[]</h3>
<p>Value: Reference: #/$defs/exclusion.</p>
<p>All of the following constraints apply:</p>
<p>When <code>state</code> is &quot;invalidated&quot;:</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>members</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>array</td>
<td>Invalidated corpora have no executable memberships.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>manifest_sha256</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>null</td>
<td>Assignment-bearing digest is cleared after invalidation.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>manifest_artifact</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>null</td>
<td>Assignment-bearing artifact is cleared after invalidation.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>preparation</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>null</td>
<td>Assignment-bearing preparation is cleared after invalidation.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
<h3 id="definition-selection">Definition: selection</h3>
<p>Exact effective selection recipe, allowing automatic corpus selection without segment-by-segment approval.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>recipe_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>
<td>Stable selection recipe identity.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>recipe_revision</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td>string</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>preset_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>attribution_bases</code></td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>source_asset_ids</code></td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>languages</code></td>

<td></td>

</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>explicitly_excluded_segment_ids</code></td>

<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>options</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>

<td>Adapter-specific filters and quality thresholds validated by the selected preset/adapter.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Namespaced custom selection rules with documented adapter interpretation.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>

<td>Training step reported by the adapter.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>format</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Declared checkpoint serialization or provider format.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>artifact</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>All listed constraints</td>
<td>Optional verified checkpoint bytes.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>hosted_handle</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Optional stable nonsecret provider checkpoint identifier.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>resume_supported</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>boolean</td>
<td>Whether the producing adapter declares this checkpoint resumable.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>compatible_adapter_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Adapter identity declaring resume compatibility.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>compatible_adapter_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Compatible implementation version or range.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>base_model</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>At least one alternative</td>
<td>Required base-model identity for resume, or null when none is required.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Namespaced checkpoint compatibility facts.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointartifact">Definition: checkpoint.artifact</h3>
<p>Optional verified checkpoint bytes.</p>
<p>All of the following constraints apply:</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointartifact-alternative-1">Definition: checkpoint.artifact: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Optional verified checkpoint bytes.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/artifactReference.</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointartifact-alternative-2">Definition: checkpoint.artifact: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>sha256</code>, <code>byte_length</code>.</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointbasemodel">Definition: checkpoint.base_model</h3>
<p>Required base-model identity for resume, or null when none is required.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointbasemodel-alternative-1">Definition: checkpoint.base_model: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Exact base-model requirement for resume.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: #/$defs/baseModel.</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpointbasemodel-alternative-2">Definition: checkpoint.base_model: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpoint-alternative-1">Definition: checkpoint: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>artifact</code>.</p>
<h3 id="definition-checkpoint-alternative-2">Definition: checkpoint: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>hosted_handle</code>.</p>
<h3 id="definition-licensedeclaration">Definition: licenseDeclaration</h3>

<h3 id="definition-licensedeclarationattribution-alternative-1">Definition: licenseDeclaration.attribution: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Required or supplied attribution text.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="definition-licensedeclarationattribution-alternative-2">Definition: licenseDeclaration.attribution: Alternative 2</h3>

<h3 id="definition-evaluation">Definition: evaluation</h3>

<thead>
<tr>

<td></td>

<td></td>

<h2 id="speaker-audio-segment-catalog">Speaker audio segment catalog</h2>
<p>Portable current speaker-evidence references across the workspace library. Embedded Cueson owns cue timing and local attribution; current catalog mappings own optional known-person identity.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/speaker-segments.schema.json">Download speaker-segments.schema.json</a></p>

<h3 id="speakerfilter">speaker_filter</h3>
<p>Requested speaker identity, or null for an unfiltered segment result.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="speakerfilter-alternative-1">speaker_filter: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Catalog speaker requested by this query.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid.</p>
<h3 id="speakerfilter-alternative-2">speaker_filter: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>
<h3 id="segments">segments</h3>
<p>Ordered source-linked segment records; an empty query result is valid.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="segments-1">segments[]</h3>
<p>Value: Reference: #/$defs/segment.</p>
<h3 id="nextcursor">next_cursor</h3>
<p>Pagination continuation token, or null when complete.</p>
<p>At least one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="nextcursor-alternative-1">next_cursor: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Opaque continuation cursor without secrets.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<h3 id="nextcursor-alternative-2">next_cursor: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Value: null.</p>
<h3 id="definition-diagnostic">Definition: diagnostic</h3>
<p>A nonsecret method-qualified processing or selection finding.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>code</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Stable diagnostic code.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>severity</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>&quot;info&quot;, &quot;warning&quot;, &quot;error&quot;</td>
<td>Severity of the technical diagnostic.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>message</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Human-readable explanation without credentials or storage paths.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>method</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Method or adapter that produced this finding.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>method_version</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Version of the producing method.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>details</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>object</td>
<td>Adapter-specific structured diagnostic measurements; must not contain secrets or local storage locations.</td>
<td></td>

<p>All of the following constraints apply:</p>
<p>When <code>mode</code> is &quot;reuse&quot;:</p>
<p>Required fields: <code>python_path</code>.</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>offline_assets</code>.</p>
<p>When <code>mode</code> is &quot;managed&quot;:</p>
<p>Disallowed combination: has <code>python_path</code>.</p>

<h2 id="portable-workspace-backup">Portable workspace backup</h2>
<p>Completed portable authority bundle with consistent catalog proof, current immutable byte inventory and explicit retention dependencies. Runtime validates digests, relations and store availability in addition to this structural contract.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/workspace-backup.schema.json">Download workspace-backup.schema.json</a></p>

<h2 id="insonic-workspace-configuration">insonic workspace configuration</h2>
<p>Versioned nonsecret configuration selecting artifact, catalog and graph adapters independently. Filesystem/SQLite/LadybugDB are defaults, while S3/PostgreSQL/ArcadeDB have full planned application support.</p>
<p><a href="/schemas/v1.0.1/workspace-config.schema.json">Download workspace-config.schema.json</a></p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>schema_version</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;1.0.1&quot;</td>
<td>Version of this public insonic contract.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>kind</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;workspace-config&quot;</td>
<td>Document discriminator selected by the master registry.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>workspace_id</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>
<td>Stable workspace scope used by all domain records.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>display_name</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>User-facing workspace name.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>control_directory</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Selected local directory for control files, scratch and caches even when authority is remote.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>profiles</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>object</td>
<td>Three independent required backend selections.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>default_import_timezone</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/timezone</td>
<td>Default timezone captured when creating an import batch.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>extensions</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/extensions</td>
<td>Optional namespaced nonsecret extension data.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>credential_namespace_id</code></td>
<td>No</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/uuid</td>
<td>Optional nonsecret local credential-store namespace, defaulting to workspace_id. Restore preserves the independently configured destination namespace when adopting source catalog identity; it never copies credential values.</td>

<td>Exactly one alternative</td>
<td>Nonsecret adapter configuration matching adapter_id; conditional rules identify the exact permitted shape.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td>No</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>Optional tested backend version constraint; health/capability checks verify it before use.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<h2 id="shared-definitions">Shared definitions</h2>
<p>Shared logical types for the v1.0.1 contract. This resource defines reusable values and is not a standalone application document envelope. Format annotations require a format-aware validator; semantic comparisons and timezone resolution remain application validation.</p>
<h3 id="definition-uuid">Definition: uuid</h3>
<p>Stable application-generated UUID identity. UUIDs identify logical records, not database row IDs or filesystem locations.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-sha256">Definition: sha256</h3>
<p>Lowercase SHA-256 digest of the complete immutable byte sequence. Provider ETags and multipart/composite checksums are not this digest.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-revision">Definition: revision</h3>
<p>Monotonically increasing logical revision within one entity or ordered revision stream. Expected-revision checks prevent lost updates; database-native sequences are not exposed as identity.</p>
<p>Value: integer.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-number">1</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="cueson-compatible-timestamp">Cueson-compatible timestamp</h3>
<p>Absolute instant in Cueson's timestamp format: RFC 3339 text and Unix nanoseconds representing the same instant. The application verifies agreement and preserves integer precision; import wall-time literals and media-relative intervals use distinct types.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>iso</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>string</td>
<td>RFC 3339 representation of the instant, including its UTC offset and available fractional-second precision. Audit output uses UTC.</td>
<td>&quot;2026-10-04T18:30:00.123456Z&quot;; &quot;1970-01-01T00:00:00.123456789Z&quot;</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>unix_ns</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>integer</td>
<td>Nanoseconds since the Unix epoch for the same instant as iso. Parse and serialize this integer without floating-point rounding; JSON Schema validation alone does not verify agreement with iso.</td>
<td>123456789</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;iso&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;1970-01-01T00:00:00.123456789Z&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;unix_ns&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">123456789</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-calendardate">Definition: calendarDate</h3>
<p>Calendar date with day precision, formatted YYYY-MM-DD. A date does not assert a known recording time.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;2026-10-04&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-walltimestamp">Definition: wallTimestamp</h3>
<p>Entered timestamp literal with seconds and up to nanosecond precision, Z or numeric offset. Without a zone, the captured import timezone policy applies. Calendar validity, DST folds/gaps and zone-offset agreement require application validation.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;2026-10-04T14:30:00&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;2026-10-04T18:30:00Z&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-timezone">Definition: timezone</h3>
<p>Timezone selector: local, UTC, an IANA identifier or a numeric offset. This pattern validates shape, not IANA membership. Resolve local once and persist its actual interpretation.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;local&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;UTC&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;America/New_York&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;+02:00&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-mediainterval">Definition: mediaInterval</h3>
<p>Positive-duration, half-open original-media interval in integer microseconds. The application must additionally validate end_us &gt; start_us and probed-duration/codec tolerance.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>
<th scope="col">Required</th>
<th scope="col">Value</th>
<th scope="col">Description</th>
<th scope="col">Examples</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>

<h3 id="definition-credentialid">Definition: credentialId</h3>
<p>Opaque credential ID resolved by SecretStore. This field never contains a password, API key, session token or secret-bearing connection URL.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-endpoint">Definition: endpoint</h3>
<p>Configured HTTP(S) service endpoint without URL user information, query tokens or fragments. Local HTTP is permitted explicitly; service credentials are separate opaque references.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;https://objects.example.com&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;http://127.0.0.1:2480&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-nonsecretoptions">Definition: nonsecretOptions</h3>
<p>Adapter-specific, versioned options validated further against that adapter's capability/configuration schema. These properties are intentionally extensible and must not contain credential values.</p>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;sample_rate&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-number">16000</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;device&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;cpu&quot;</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="definition-extensions">Definition: extensions</h3>
<p>Optional namespaced extension data. Core consumers may preserve unknown extensions without interpreting them. Extensions must not redefine required fields, hide credentials or mutate immutable provenance.</p>
<p>Value: object.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-punctuation">{</span>
  <span class="hljs-attr">&quot;example.org/note&quot;</span><span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> <span class="hljs-string">&quot;Optional annotation&quot;</span>
<span class="hljs-punctuation">}</span>
</code></pre>
<h3 id="consumer-speaker-identifier">Consumer speaker identifier</h3>
<p>Exact bounded recording-local speaker token. It is not a global person identity.</p>
<p>Value: string.</p>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;Alex&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;f7746aa9-d48c-49ba-9f26-9c30219b1029&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;日本語&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;é&quot;</span>
</code></pre>
<p>Example:</p>
<pre tabindex="0" aria-label="Code example"><code class="language-json"><span class="hljs-string">&quot;🙂&quot;</span>
</code></pre>

<h3 id="definition-modelresolutiontarget">Definition: modelResolution.target</h3>
<p>Exact consumer selection, operation-neutral acquisition selection or explicitly unresolved missing diagnosis.</p>
<p>Exactly one of the following alternatives must match:</p>
<h3 id="definition-modelresolutiontarget-alternative-1">Definition: modelResolution.target: Alternative 1</h3>
<p>Operation-specific exact target.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/modelTarget.</p>
<h3 id="definition-modelresolutiontarget-alternative-2">Definition: modelResolution.target: Alternative 2</h3>
<p>Explicit bundle-acquisition target without an inference election.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/modelAcquisitionTarget.</p>
<h3 id="definition-modelresolutiontarget-alternative-3">Definition: modelResolution.target: Alternative 3</h3>
<p>Missing consumer reference diagnosis without an invented identity.</p>
<p>Value: Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/modelUnresolvedTarget.</p>
<h3 id="definition-modelresolutiondiagnostics">Definition: modelResolution.diagnostics</h3>
<p>Separate diagnoses; compatibility does not claim acoustic qualification.</p>
<p>Value: array.</p>
<h3 id="definition-modelresolutiondiagnostics-1">Definition: modelResolution.diagnostics[]</h3>
<p>Actionable compatibility/availability diagnosis without credential values.</p>

<h3 id="definition-modelunresolvedtarget">Definition: modelUnresolvedTarget</h3>
<p>Response-only missing-selection diagnosis. It never fabricates a model ID or authorizes execution.</p>
<div class="table-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable table" tabindex="0"><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th scope="col">Field</th>

</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td><code>kind</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Constant &quot;unresolved&quot;</td>
<td>The reference could not establish an exact valid consumer election.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>operation</code></td>
<td>Yes</td>
<td>Reference: common.schema.json#/$defs/modelOperation</td>
<td>Intended stage operation retained with the unavailable reference.</td>
<td></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
</div>
<p>The current runtime-response contract permits a structured result alongside an error, so failed waits retain terminal work details. All CLI machine routes use this envelope, fixed stream/exit rules and exact integer serialization. Persisted 1.0.0 workspace/tool/document readers select their historical packaged contracts; runtime IPC and installed-package identities remain current-version contracts. Historical accepted bytes and hashes are retained. See <a href="/docs/v1.0.1/cli/">CLI invocation and results</a>.</p>
<p>The registered backend-plan contract describes nonsecret initial selection for all six backends. Shared bootstrap generates workspace/profile identities and revisions; generated workspace configuration is accepted unchanged for idempotent retries. The query-assistance contract includes native Ollama configuration, metadata discovery and elected synthetic testing. Provider contract version 1 identifies insonic's translation contract. Secrets remain in separate protected input.</p>
